<script type="text/javascript">
    function submitForm() {
    $.ajax({
    	type:'POST', 
    	url: 'newsletter/signup.php', 
    	data:$('#newsletterForm').serialize(), 
    	success: function(response) {
    		$('#data').append('<p class="error"><strong>Success!</strong> Thank you for signing up.</p>');
        	$('#newsletterForm').find('.form_result').html(response);
    	}
    });

    return false;
}
</script>
